d13fad810819749fa5629d0f093da5edd47f1210: $$A_{1}v_{1}=A_{2}v_{2}$$

478274f9452fd1435f65b73870384f32c9363e8d: $$L=\Delta K$$

d472109d1b48a01d5fc233869f306acfa4ee493c: $$\Delta K=K_{1}-K_{i}=K_{2}-K_{1}=\frac{1}{2}\Delta mv_{2}^2-\frac{1}{2}\Delta mv_{1}^2$$

957c2df59342571ccc7e1da1925db4d0a87ac162: $$\frac{1}{2}\Delta m(v_{2}^2-v_{1}^\text{2})=\frac{1}{2}\rho\Delta V(v_{2}^\text{2}-v_{1}^2)=\Delta K$$

3ff2a09ea33496fb3665fd06ca8d8fdcd6d02d5b: $$L=L_{p}+L_{g}$$

5782ed78b94752a5c80f7ac059d55c03175d35f9: $$L=-E_{pot}=\Delta mgh(y_{2}-y_{1})=-\rho \Delta V \cdot g(y_{2}-y_{1})$$

d556358775901a190afc3b45065b61e0da3ea7c7: $$P\cdot A=Fp$$

70356d3b06d646d6eb8ef9a7f12c1cff8741ba33: $$P=\frac{F}{A}$$

a8ce75ebbb386a7430a131f9f26eaea1000522d7: $$L_{p}=P_{1}\Delta V-P_{2}\Delta V=(P_{1}-P_{2})\Delta V$$

0909a35ec38e6feb6d79ffe649f04979db5ff17d: $$(P_{1}-P_{2})\Delta V-\rho\Delta Vg(y_{2}-y_{1})=\frac{1}{2}\rho\Delta V(v_{2}^\text{2}-v_{1}^2)$$

5ccabf46eea0db9b75ffba94ba81dc3bd0c05dbc: $$P_{1}+\rho gy_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\rho v^2_{1}=P_{2}+\rho gy_{2}+\frac{1}{2}\rho v_{2}^\text{2}$$

e623badc8855a56976c7dda49a7cb63ec8fcdc73: $$P+\frac{1}{2}\rho v^2+\rho gh=\cos t$$

0e7d4c7d03ce426e3a4091c8c535da670bd847c6: $$v=70 \frac{m}{s}$$
